1	# Plan — yf MCP `get_ratios` HTML fallback (ABA-72)
2	
3	**Spec:** `docs/design-docs/yf-ratios-html-fallback/design-doc.md` (status: accepted, crit-approved 2026-05-13)
4	**Linear:** ABA-72
5	**Scope:** single function in `mcp/yf/tools.py` + new helpers + new dep + tests.
6	
7	## Dependency graph
8	
9	```
10	Slice 1 (spike + parser) ──┬─→ Slice 3 (wire fallback in get_ratios)
11	Slice 2 (retry helper) ────┘                  │
12	                                              ↓
13	                                       Slice 4 (fitness tests)
14	                                              │
15	                                              ↓
16	                                       Slice 5 (deps + CI)
17	```
18	
19	Slices 1 and 2 are independent and can run in parallel; both feed Slice 3. Slice 4 codifies Slice 3 behaviour. Slice 5 finalises packaging.
20	
21	## Vertical slicing rationale
22	
23	Each slice ends with a runnable, demonstrable artefact. We avoid horizontal layering (e.g. "all parsers first, then all callers"); each slice exercises a real call path.
24	
25	---
26	
27	## Slice 1 — KSPI spike: fetch + parse Yahoo HTML pages
28	
29	**Goal:** resolve Open Question 1 (does `financialCurrency` appear on the HTML page?) and produce a working `_ratios_from_html(ticker)` helper for KSPI.
30	
31	**Work:**
32	- Fetch `https://finance.yahoo.com/quote/KSPI/key-statistics/` with `curl_cffi` (`impersonate="chrome"`). Plain `requests` / `curl` get 404 — Yahoo bot-detects by TLS fingerprint. (Resolved in spike 2026-05-13.)
33	- Parse with `BeautifulSoup`. Implement `_parse_valuation_table(soup)` that targets `<section data-testid="qsp-statistics"> > .table-container > table` (first table in the section) and returns a `{label → Current value}` dict using the "Current" column (index 1).
34	- Extract from the table: `pe_ratio` (Trailing P/E), `ps_ratio` (Price/Sales), `marketCap` (Market Cap), `enterpriseValue` (Enterprise Value), `ev_revenue` (Enterprise Value/Revenue), `ev_ebitda` (Enterprise Value/EBITDA). Convert "16.24B" / "189.88M" / "2.42T" strings to floats via a small `_parse_si_number` helper.
35	- Open Question 1 already resolved in the spike (HTML page does not surface `financialCurrency`; read it from `Ticker.info` on the wired path).
36	- Define internal `_ScrapeError` exception.
37	
38	**Acceptance criteria:**
39	- `_ratios_from_html("KSPI")` returns a dict with all required fields populated.
40	- Returned `pe_ratio`, `ps_ratio`, `currentPrice` are within ±2% of values manually read off the Yahoo page at the same moment.
41	- Open Question 1 is answered in writing (in the design doc) — either "`reporting_currency` is read from element X" or "no currency indicator on the page; default to None and document".
42	
43	**Verification:**
44	- Manual REPL from `mcp/yf/`: `.venv/bin/python -c "from tools import _ratios_from_html; print(_ratios_from_html('KSPI'))"` prints populated dict.
45	- Eyeball comparison vs. live Yahoo page.
46	
47	**Not in this slice:** retry logic, integration into `get_ratios`, tests as CI fitness functions.
48	
49	---
50	
51	## Slice 2 — `_fetch_with_retry` helper
52	
53	**Goal:** isolate the retry policy in one tested helper. Independent of parsing.
54	
55	**Work:**
56	- Implement `_fetch_with_retry(url, attempts=3, timeout=5.0)` returning a `curl_cffi` response.
57	- Retry on: `curl_cffi.requests.errors.RequestsError` with `code` matching connection/timeout, HTTP 5xx, HTTP 429. Backoff: 0.5s, 1.0s, 2.0s.
58	- Do NOT retry on: other 4xx, parse failures (parse failures don't reach this helper anyway).
59	- Raise `_ScrapeError` after exhausting retries.
60	
61	**Acceptance criteria:**
62	- Helper retries exactly the documented set of transient errors.
63	- Helper does not retry non-transient 4xx (e.g. 403, 404).
64	- Total wall-clock for 3-retry exhaustion bounded by ~3.5s of sleeps + 3×5s timeouts ≤ 12s (matches NFR row 3).
65	
66	**Verification:**
67	- Pytest with `requests_mock` or `responses`: 200 first try → 1 call; 500/500/200 → 3 calls, total elapsed > 1.5s sleep; 500/500/500 → raises after 3 calls; 404 → raises after 1 call (no retry); 429/200 → 2 calls.
68	
69	---
70	
71	## Checkpoint 1 — after Slices 1 + 2
72	
73	Smoke test: hand-call `_fetch_with_retry` against the real Yahoo KSPI page, pass to `_ratios_from_html`. Confirm dict matches Slice 1 output. **Human review** of the answer to Open Question 1 before locking the response schema in Slice 3.
74	
75	---
76	
77	## Slice 3 — Wire fallback into `get_ratios`
78	
79	**Goal:** the user-visible behaviour change. `get_ratios("KSPI")` returns populated data; `get_ratios("AAPL")` is unchanged.
80	
81	**Work:**
82	- Extract current `get_ratios` body into `_ratios_from_info(ticker, info)`. Add `source: "yahoo_api"` and `reporting_currency: info.get("financialCurrency")`.
83	- Add the fallback branch:
84	  ```python
85	  if not ps_ratio:
86	      try:
87	          return _ratios_from_html(ticker)
88	      except _ScrapeError as e:
89	          raise YFNoDataError(...) from e
90	  ```
91	- Add `source: "yahoo_html"` and `reporting_currency` (per Slice 1 finding) to the HTML path return.
92	- Add the three new stderr log lines from the Operability section.
93	
94	**Acceptance criteria:**
95	- `get_ratios("AAPL")` returns identical numeric fields to today (golden fixture) plus `source: "yahoo_api"`.
96	- `get_ratios("KSPI")` returns populated `pe_ratio`, `ps_ratio`, `currentPrice`, `marketCap`, `source: "yahoo_html"`.
97	- `get_ratios("CLEARLY_NOT_A_TICKER")` raises `YFNoDataError` (loud failure preserved).
98	- Stderr logs match the three lines documented in Operability.
99	
100	**Verification:**
101	- Manual from `mcp/yf/`: `.venv/bin/python -c "from tools import get_ratios; print(get_ratios('KSPI'))"` and `... 'AAPL'`.
102	- Run `/signal KSPI` end-to-end — workflow completes through the ratios step.
103	
104	---
105	
106	## Checkpoint 2 — after Slice 3
107	
108	End-to-end smoke: `/signal KSPI` and `/signal AAPL` both produce reports without error. **Human review** before codifying the behaviour as CI fitness functions (so we lock the right behaviour, not a buggy first cut).
109	
110	---
111	
112	## Slice 4 — Fitness functions (the 7 tests from the NFR table)
113	
114	**Goal:** every NFR in the design doc has an automated check.
115	
116	**Work:** add `mcp/yf/tests/test_get_ratios.py` with:
117	
118	1. `test_get_ratios_latency` — `get_ratios("AAPL")` p95 < 1.0s (run 5×, take median to dampen noise; doc as nightly).
119	2. `test_get_ratios_html_fallback_latency` — `get_ratios("KSPI")` < 3.0s when first HTTP attempt succeeds.
120	3. `test_get_ratios_html_retry_budget` — mock 3 transient failures; assert total elapsed < 12s and 3 attempts fired.
121	4. `test_get_ratios_kspi_fallback_values` — `get_ratios("KSPI")` returns fields within ±2% of recorded fixture.
122	5. `test_get_ratios_us_megacap_unchanged` — golden fixture for AAPL/MSFT/NVDA, `source: "yahoo_api"`, numeric fields match within tolerance.
123	6. `test_get_ratios_html_malformed_raises` — feed parser stub HTML with renamed table rows; assert `YFNoDataError` (loud, not silent `None`-everywhere).
124	7. (impl-detail) `test_fetch_with_retry_*` — the Slice 2 retry tests live here.
125	
126	**Acceptance criteria:**
127	- All tests pass locally (`pytest mcp/yf/tests/`).
128	- Tests requiring live Yahoo are marked `@pytest.mark.nightly` (so the every-commit CI run stays hermetic; nightly run hits the network).
129	
130	**Verification:**
131	- `pytest mcp/yf/tests/ -m "not nightly"` green in the hermetic path.
132	- `pytest mcp/yf/tests/` green including live calls.
133	
134	---
135	
136	## Slice 5 — Dependencies and CI wiring
137	
138	**Goal:** the new code is reproducibly installable and the fitness functions actually run.
139	
140	**Work:**
141	- `mcp/yf/requirements.txt`: add `beautifulsoup4>=4.12` and `curl_cffi>=0.7`. Bare `requests` is insufficient — Yahoo bot-blocks it at the TLS layer for sub-pages.
142	- Add a `pytest` test-dep section if `requirements.txt` doesn't already have one, or add `mcp/yf/requirements-dev.txt`.
143	- If `.github/workflows/` exists, add or extend a workflow to run the hermetic tests on every push and the nightly tests on a schedule. If not, document the manual command in `mcp/yf/README.md` (single-user pack — CI is nice-to-have, not blocking).
144	
145	**Acceptance criteria:**
146	- `pip install -r mcp/yf/requirements.txt` in a clean venv resolves cleanly.
147	- `pip-audit` reports no known CVEs against the new dep.
148	- CI workflow (if added) is green.
149	
150	**Verification:**
151	- Clean-venv install test (Python 3.11+).
152	- Push to a feature branch and confirm Actions run green.
153	
154	---
155	
156	## Final checkpoint — pre-merge
157	
158	- All slices verified.
159	- Linear ABA-72 set to In Progress at start of Slice 1, Done after merge.
160	- Smoke test `/signal KSPI` one more time on `main` after merge.
161	- Design doc status flipped from `draft` → `accepted`.
162	- Linear wave promotion check (per CLAUDE.md) — find issues blocked by ABA-72 and promote 1–3.
163	
164	## Risks and mitigations
165	
166	| Risk | Mitigation |
167	|---|---|
168	| Slice 1 discovers `financialCurrency` isn't on the HTML page | Acceptable per design doc — `reporting_currency: None` is documented behaviour; document the finding and move on. |
169	| Yahoo HTML changes mid-build | Fitness function `test_get_ratios_html_malformed_raises` catches structural changes; if live KSPI breaks during build, retry once then escalate. |
170	| `beautifulsoup4` resolves an unexpected sub-dep | Slice 5 pip-audit and clean-venv install gate this. |
171	| Latency NFRs flake in CI | All latency tests measure median of N runs, not single-shot p95. Nightly-only so a single bad run doesn't block merges. |
172	
173	## Out of scope (explicit non-goals — do not let scope creep in)
174	
175	- Currency normalisation across `get_financials` / `get_estimates` (deferred per design doc).
176	- Skill-side manual-input fallback (ABA-74 — separate ticket).
177	- EDGAR SBC fallback (ABA-75 — separate ticket).
178	- UA-rotation / proxy pool for 429 mitigation (premature; not in design).
179	- Caching scraped values across processes (premature).
180

on AMZN — base line bends harder (1.46 → 0.18); on NVDA — confirms similar pattern.\n\n---\n\n### Slice 5 — FCF margin trajectory chart\n**Files:** new `ui/src/components/charts/FcfMarginTrajectory.jsx`.\n\n**Data flow:**\n- Historical points from `stages.model.historical_fcf_margins[]` (3–4 years), plotted as two thin lines (clean vs reported).\n- TTM point from `fcf_margin_ttm` and `fcf_margin_ttm_reported`.\n- Y1→Y5 extension: flat at `fcf_margin_ttm` (or playbook's `terminal_margin` if surfaced — out of v1 unless it lands in JSON; default flat).\n- Gap between clean and reported lines shaded to visualise SBC drag.\n\n**Acceptance criteria:**\n- Fallback when `historical_fcf_margins` absent: render TTM-only with a small note \"3y history unavailable — re-run /stock-model with v1.11+ for trajectory\".\n- Negative margins handled (AMZN clean TTM = −1.6%) — Y-axis spans signed range without breaking.\n- No render for pre-profit.\n\n**Verification:** Model tab on AMZN — clean line dips below historical due to capex AI cycle; META — clean and reported lines closer together (smaller SBC drag); NVDA — both lines high, narrow gap.\n\n---\n\n### Slice 6 — Scenario fan chart (per-share single axis)\n**Files:** new `ui/src/components/charts/ScenarioFan.jsx`.\n\n**Data flow per scenario:**\n- Year FCF series: `y1_fcf` then iterated `× (1 + y2_5_cagr)` for Y2–Y5.\n- Divide each by `stages.model.shares_diluted` → FCF/share/year.\n- Terminal IV marker = `scenarios.{s}.intrinsic_value_per_share` at Y5 (single ◆ marker per scenario).\n- Horizontal reference line at `stages.model.current_price`.\n\n**Acceptance criteria:**\n- Three bands (bear/base/bull) fanning out across Y1–Y5; per-share IV diamond markers at Y5; current-price line spans full chart.\n- Sanity check: terminal markers ordered bear < base < bull; current_price line position visually matches `range_vs_price` text on the tab.\n- No render for pre-profit.\n\n**Verification:** Model tab on META — current $614 line crosses between bear ($444) and base ($1155) markers, matching `WITHIN BEAR-BASE` badge. AMZN — current $264 line above base IV $176 marker, matching `WITHIN BASE-BULL`.\n\n---\n\n### Slice 7 — Captions, styling, docs\n**Files:** `ui/src/styles.css`; `ui/src/components/charts/*.jsx` for caption text; `DESIGN.md` for the UI Model-tab note; PR description.\n\n**Acceptance criteria:**\n- One-sentence caption under each chart that explains the *thesis implication*, not the chart mechanics. The plan breaks ABA-115 into **7 vertical slices** with explicit gates between phases:

1. **Slice 1** — Model-tab shell (UI; no SKILL change required)

what skill (if any) should i use to execute the implementation

> AGENT

`agent-skills:build` per slice is the natural pair to `agent-skills:plan` — it's literally "implement the next task incrementally — build, test, verify, commit." Invoke it once at the start of each slice; it picks the next unchecked item from `tasks/todo.md`, drives the work, gates against the slice's acceptance criteria, and commits at the slice boundary.

Two refinements worth knowing:

- **Slices 1, 4, 5, 6, 7** are UI-heavy. Inside the `build` flow, `agent-skills:frontend-ui-engineering` is the right lens for component structure / accessibility / production-quality rigor — `build` will reach for it itself or you can name it explicitly.
- **Slice 3** (re-run covered tickers) isn't really an engineering build — it's just invoking `/stock-signal` and `/stock-model` four times and diffing JSON. No build skill needed; just run them.

Heads-up that this repo has no automated test runner (per `DESIGN.md`/AGENTS), so `build`'s "verify with tests" step collapses to manual smoke tests + JSON-diff checks. That's already encoded in each slice's Verification block.

Recommended kickoff: `/agent-skills:build` with Slice 1 as the target.

UI renders the three visuals for any covered ticker's latest `reports/TICKER_YYYYMMDD.json` where `stages.model` is present.\n2. Shows trailing-3y CAGR transitioning to projected Y1–Y5 CAGR per scenario, annotated where the growth cap fires.\n\n## Files to touch\n\n- `ui/src/components/charts/CagrGlidepath.jsx` — new file (hand-rolled SVG, no chart deps)\n- `ui/src/components/ModelReport.jsx` — mount the chart (ESTABLISHED branch only)\n- `ui/src/styles.css` — chart container styles + cap-callout footnote\n\n## Data flow\n\n- **Trailing-3y CAGR** (single point on left): `stages.model.growth_rate.trailing_3y_cagr`\n- **Y1 implicit CAGR** per scenario (back-derived client-side): `(scenarios[s].y1_fcf / stages.model.fcf_ttm) − 1`\n- **Y2–Y5 CAGR** per scenario (flat through Y2–Y5): `scenarios[s].y2_5_cagr`\n- **Cap annotation** (when `growth_rate.cap_applied === true`): surfaces `applied_base_cagr` and `cap_source`. One canonical phrasing template, e.g. *\"capped from 145.7% by fallback_ceiling (18%)\"*. Pin the template so all `cap_source` values render consistently.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- Three lines (bear / base / bull) colour-coded; small legend at the top.\n- At every year Y2–Y5: bear < base < bull (monotone-ordered).\n- Cap footnote rendered for any run where `growth_rate.cap_applied === true`; absent otherwise.\n- No render when `stages.model.method` starts with `\"pre-profit\"`.\n\n## Verification\n\n`npm run dev` → Model tab on:\n\n- **META** — base line bends at Y2 with cap callout firing\n- **AMZN** — base line bends hardest (trailing 1.46 → applied 0.18)\n- **NVDA** — confirms similar capped pattern\n- **RDDT** — chart NOT rendered\n\n## Slice independence\n\n[4/7], [5/7], [6/7] can ship in any order once [3/7] is green."
}

Shows trailing-3y CAGR transitioning to projected Y1–Y5 CAGR per scenario, annotated where the growth cap fires.\n\n## Files to touch\n\n* `ui/src/components/charts/CagrGlidepath.jsx` — new file (hand-rolled SVG, no chart deps)\n* `ui/src/components/ModelReport.jsx` — mount the chart (ESTABLISHED branch only)\n* `ui/src/styles.css` — chart container styles + cap-callout footnote\n\n## Data flow\n\n* **Trailing-3y CAGR** (single point on left): `stages.model.growth_rate.trailing_3y_cagr`\n* **Y1 implicit CAGR** per scenario (back-derived client-side): `(scenarios[s].y1_fcf / stages.model.fcf_ttm) − 1`\n* **Y2–Y5 CAGR** per scenario (flat through Y2–Y5): `scenarios[s].y2_5_cagr`\n* **Cap annotation** (when `growth_rate.cap_applied === true`): surfaces `applied_base_cagr` and `cap_source`. One canonical phrasing template, e.g. *\"capped from 145.7% by fallback_ceiling (18%)\"*. Pin the template so all `cap_source` values render consistently.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n* Three lines (bear / base / bull) colour-coded; small legend at the top.\n* At every year Y2–Y5: bear < base < bull (monotone-ordered).\n* Cap footnote rendered for any run where `growth_rate.cap_applied === true`; absent otherwise.\n* No render when `stages.model.method` starts with `\"pre-profit\"`.\n\n## Verification\n\n`npm run dev` → Model tab on:\n\n* **META** — base line bends at Y2 with cap callout firing\n* **AMZN** — base line bends hardest (trailing 1.46 → applied 0.18)\n* **NVDA** — confirms similar capped pattern\n* **RDDT** — chart NOT rendered\n\n## Slice independence\n\n\\[4/7\\], \\[5/7\\], \\[6/7\\] can ship in any order once \\[3/7\\] is green.","priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-138/47-cagr-glidepath-chart","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-138-47-cagr-glidepath-chart","createdAt":"2026-05-19T05:05:26.746Z","updatedAt":"2026-05-19T05:05:26.746Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Todo","statusType":"unstarted","labels":[],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","assignee":"Anton Babushkin","assigneeId":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Equity skill pack","projectId":"553fa5bd-f8cc-4a58-8236-2df12aa049d5","parentId":"ABA-115","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1","cycleId":"e73f8fce-ad06-49ee-8ea1-67f77aed857a"}

  "title": "[5/7] FCF margin trajectory chart",
  "team": "c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1",

  "description": "**Parent:** ABA-115\n**Plan:** `tasks/plan.md` → Slice 5\n**Skill to use:** `/agent-skills:build` with UI lens `agent-skills:frontend-ui-engineering`\n**Depends on:** [3/7] (real `historical_fcf_margins[]` series needed for the historical portion)\n\n## Goal\n\nFCF margin trajectory chart — surfaces the \"base case assumes today's clean FCF margin holds flat\" assumption. Two thin lines (clean vs reported) across 3y history → TTM → flat Y1→Y5; SBC drag visualised as the gap between them.\n\n## Files to touch\n\n- `ui/src/components/charts/FcfMarginTrajectory.jsx` — new file\n- `ui/src/components/ModelReport.jsx` — mount (ESTABLISHED branch only)\n- `ui/src/styles.css` — additions if needed\n\n## Data flow\n\n- **Historical points** (3–4y): `stages.model.historical_fcf_margins[]` (clean + reported per year)\n- **TTM points**: `stages.model.fcf_margin_ttm` (clean) and `stages.model.fcf_margin_ttm_reported` (reported)\n- **Y1→Y5 extension**: flat at `fcf_margin_ttm` (if a playbook `terminal_margin` is surfaced in JSON later, that's a follow-up — flat-line is v1)\n- **SBC drag**: shade the area between the clean and reported lines\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- When `historical_fcf_margins[]` is absent (old pre-v1.11 report): render TTM-only with a small note *\"3y history unavailable — re-run /stock-model with v1.11+ for trajectory\"*. Do not crash.\n- Negative margins handled cleanly: AMZN's clean TTM is **−1.64%** — Y-axis must span signed range without breaking layout.\n- AMZN edge case: plot the **unmodified** `fcf_margin_ttm` (−1.64%), NOT the `fcf_margin_normalized` (7.74%). The chart's purpose is to surface the gap that justified normalisation, not hide it.\n- No render when `stages.model.method` starts with `\"pre-profit\"`.\n\n## Verification\n\n`npm run dev` → Model tab on:\n\n- **AMZN** — clean line dips into negative; SBC drag gap visible\n- **META** — clean and reported lines close together (small SBC drag)\n- **NVDA** — both lines high, narrow gap\n- **(Optional) RDDT** — chart NOT rendered (pre-profit guard)\n\n## Slice independence\n\n[4/7], [5/7], [6/7] can ship in any order once [3/7] is green."
}
```

{
  "title": "[6/7] Scenario fan chart (per-share single axis)",
  "team": "c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1",
  "project": "553fa5bd-f8cc-4a58-8236-2df12aa049d5",
  "parentId": "ABA-115",
  "cycle": "e73f8fce-ad06-49ee-8ea1-67f77aed857a",
  "assignee": "me",
  "priority": 3,
  "description": "**Parent:** ABA-115\n**Plan:** `tasks/plan.md` → Slice 6\n**Skill to use:** `/agent-skills:build` with UI lens `agent-skills:frontend-ui-engineering`\n**Depends on:** [3/7] (charts need refreshed reports)\n\n## Goal\n\nScenario fan chart — projected FCF/share Y1→Y5 for bear/base/bull, with per-share terminal IV markers and the current-price reference line, all on a **single per-share axis**.\n\n## Files to touch\n\n- `ui/src/components/charts/ScenarioFan.jsx` — new file\n- `ui/src/components/ModelReport.jsx` — mount (ESTABLISHED branch only)\n- `ui/src/styles.css` — diamond marker + reference-line styles\n\n## Data flow per scenario\n\n- **Year FCF series**: `y1_fcf` then iterated `× (1 + y2_5_cagr)` for Y2–Y5\n- **Convert to per-share**: divide every year by `stages.model.shares_diluted` (raw count, not millions — see Risk #2)\n- **Terminal IV marker**: `scenarios[s].intrinsic_value_per_share` rendered as a ◆ at Y5\n- **Reference line**: horizontal at `stages.model.current_price`, spanning the full chart width\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- Three fanning bands (bear / base / bull) across Y1–Y5; three IV ◆ markers at Y5; horizontal current-price line.\n- Terminal IV markers ordered bear < base < bull at Y5.\n- Current-price line position visually matches `range_vs_price` text on the tab (e.g. line *between* bear and base markers when `range_vs_price === \"WITHIN BEAR-BASE\"`).\n- No render when `stages.model.method` starts with `\"pre-profit\"`.\n\n## Critical risk — magnitude check\n\n`stages.model.shares_diluted` is a **raw count** (e.g. AMZN: `10827000000` — 10.8B). Dividing by millions yields a 1000× wrong per-share fan. **Before declaring done**, hand-check: Y1 FCF/share should be in the same order of magnitude as the IV diamond markers ($1–$1000s/share, not $1000s+ and not pennies).\n\n## Verification\n\n`npm run dev` → Model tab on:\n\n- **META** — current $614 line crosses *between* bear ($444) and base ($1155) markers, matching `WITHIN BEAR-BASE` badge from the IV-range strip\n- **AMZN** — current $264 line *above* base IV $176 marker, matching `WITHIN BASE-BULL`\n- **NVDA** — confirm scenario ordering at Y5\n- **RDDT** — chart NOT rendered\n\n## Slice independence\n\n[4/7], [5/7], [6/7] can ship in any order once [3/7] is green."
}
```

<title>[1/7] Model-tab shell (UI, no SKILL change)</title>
<description>
**Parent:** <issue id="5ae08460-bbc6-42f9-94b3-14105498e30e">ABA-115</issue>
**Plan:** `tasks/plan.md` → Slice 1
**Skill to use:** `/agent-skills:build` (with `agent-skills:frontend-ui-engineering` as the UI lens — `build` will reach for it, or invoke explicitly)

## Goal

Land v1 of the Model tab — replace the *"not yet implemented"* stub with summary fields (IV-range strip, range_vs_price badge, position-sizing line, header row). No SKILL change, no charts yet. Subsumes <issue id="f6821931-3e46-4a3a-8a32-2b90ea240e1e">ABA-42</issue> v1.

## Files to touch

* `ui/src/App.jsx:64` — replace `tab === 'Model'` stub with `<ModelReport stage={stages.model} />`
* `ui/src/components/ModelReport.jsx` — new file
* `ui/src/lib/formatters.js` — add `formatCurrency`, `formatPercent`, `formatBillions`, `formatPerShare` (each returns `'—'` on null/undefined/NaN)
* `ui/src/styles.css` — Model-tab section styles, harmonised with existing `.card`/`.badge`/`.ratios` palette

## Reuse (don't reinvent)

* `verdictClass()` from `ui/src/lib/formatters.js` for the `range_vs_price` badge
* Existing `.card`, `.badge`, `.tab`, `.ratios` styles
* `ui/src/components/ScreenReport.jsx` as the component-shape pattern

## Acceptance criteria

* ESTABLISHED branch renders: header row (method, profit_stage, base WACC, NTM revenue, clean FCF margin TTM); IV-range strip showing bear / base / bull `intrinsic_value_per_share` + `current_price`; `range_vs_price` badge using existing verdict palette; position-sizing band + one-line `rationale`.
* Pre-profit branch (`method` starts with `"pre-profit"`): summary fields plus italic line *"Visual breakdown is ESTABLISHED-only for v1."*
* Reports without `stages.model` (ASML_20260517, GOOG_20260517) keep the Model tab disabled — unchanged from today.

## Verification

```bash
cd ui && npm run dev
```

Cycle the picker through AMZN_20260518, META_20260517, NVDA_20260514, RDDT_20260513 — all four render without console errors. RDDT shows the ESTABLISHED-only italic note. ASML / GOOG show Model tab disabled.

## Gate to Slice 2 (\[2/7\])

All four ticker reports render the shell correctly. If anything breaks, fix before touching the SKILL.

## Linear hygiene

When this sub-task starts, move <issue id="5ae08460-bbc6-42f9-94b3-14105498e30e">ABA-115</issue> **→ In Progress** (per AGENTS.md).
</description>

<id>5ae08460-bbc6-42f9-94b3-14105498e30e</id>
<title>/stock:model report — glidepath + scenario fan visuals</title>
<description>
## Problem

After <issue id="89e1f4f4-8b04-4c5b-9931-7f4f837de45f">ABA-110</issue> (SBC (Stock-Based Compensation) strip) and <issue id="e78e36cd-49f1-441f-96a9-317acc594230">ABA-111</issue> (growth-rate cap) landed, `/stock:model` now produces honest but **stark-looking** scenario outputs. Example META 2026-05-17:

* Bear IV (Intrinsic Value): $184
* Base IV: $393
* Bull IV: $778
* Current price: $614

Reading the base case in isolation ("$393 vs $614 → 36% overvalued") is misleading without the trajectory context. The base case mechanically applies the consensus growth ceiling in Y2 (META: 52% trailing → 18% capped), which looks like a cliff but is a smoothing artifact of the spec. A reader anchored on "base = realistic central case" will misread it as "the model thinks META is 36% overvalued" when the actual message is "the market is pricing META as a bull case — here's what has to keep going right."

## Why now

Thesis construction depends on **explaining how the numbers got where they got** — more than the numbers themselves. Without visuals, the reader has to do the unpacking in their head; with visuals, the trajectory and the asymmetry are immediately legible.

User quote (2026-05-17 session): *"reading a 'base case' that's so far from the current stock price has some context — it's unlikely that a company that has kept massive momentum in growth will suddenly stop, so this is important to put into context."*

## Scope

Add three visuals to the report UI for ESTABLISHED-path Model output:

1. **CAGR (Compound Annual Growth Rate) glidepath** — line chart showing trailing-3y CAGR (clean, SBC-stripped), then Y1→Y5 projected CAGR for each scenario (bear/base/bull). Annotate cap point ("capped from 52.1% by 18% fallback ceiling" for the base line).
2. **FCF (Free Cash Flow) margin trajectory** — area chart showing 3y historical reported vs clean FCF margin, with a constant-margin extension for Y1→Y5. Visually surfaces that base case assumes flat-line margins (no operating leverage) — and invites the user to consider whether they buy that assumption.
3. **Scenario fan chart** — cone/fan plot of Y1→Y5 FCF projection for bear/base/bull, with the corresponding terminal-year discounted-to-PV (Present Value) markers, and the current price line for reference.

## Acceptance criteria

1. UI renders the three visuals for any covered ticker's latest `reports/TICKER_YYYYMMDD.json` where `stages.model` is present.
2. Cap-point annotation reads from `stages.model.growth_rate` block (added in <issue id="e78e36cd-49f1-441f-96a9-317acc594230">ABA-111</issue>) — specifically `trailing_3y_cagr`, `applied_base_cagr`, `cap_source`.
3. FCF margin chart reads clean and reported series from `stages.model.fcf_margin_ttm` (clean) and `stages.model.fcf_margin_ttm_reported` (reported, added in <issue id="89e1f4f4-8b04-4c5b-9931-7f4f837de45f">ABA-110</issue>).
4. Scenario fan chart sources from `stages.model.scenarios.{bear,base,bull}` (existing).
5. Each visual has a one-sentence caption explaining the assumption it surfaces (not the chart mechanics — the *thesis* implication).
6. Pre-profit variant either gets its own analogous visuals or is explicitly scoped out (decide during design).

## Out of scope

* Glidepath shaping (introducing year-by-year CAGR taper instead of flat Y2-Y5) — separate ticket if worth doing
* Adding a margin-expansion lever to *base* case — separate methodology ticket if worth doing
* Sensitivity heatmap (WACC × g) — separate ticket

## Related

* Builds on <issue id="89e1f4f4-8b04-4c5b-9931-7f4f837de45f">ABA-110</issue> (SBC strip surfaces clean vs reported margins in JSON)
* Builds on <issue id="e78e36cd-49f1-441f-96a9-317acc594230">ABA-111</issue> (growth-rate block surfaces cap mechanics in JSON)
* Likely lives in Wave 4 (UI/report polish) of M5; defer until playbook layer (<issue id="70664e55-f843-4fed-8462-d8e6e78141cb">ABA-112</issue>) lands so visuals reference playbook narrative

## Methodology footnote (for the reader)

The base case is intentionally a **"stop overdelivering"** anchor:

* Margins flat at today's clean FCF margin (no operating-leverage assumption)
* Growth decelerates from trailing print to consensus-5y ceiling (or 18% fallback) in Y2
* Discount rate at WACC (Weighted Average Cost of Capital) baseline

The bull case is where margin-expansion + sustained-growth assumptions live. This is by design — it forces the thesis to articulate which levers it's betting on, rather than baking optimism into the central case.
</description>

1	import { verdictClass, ratioLabel, formatRatio } from '../lib/formatters.js';
2	
3	export default function ScreenReport({ stage }) {
4	  if (!stage) {
5	    return <p className="empty">No screen data in this report.</p>;
6	  }
7	
8	  const { verdict, profit_stage, ratios = {}, rationale } = stage;
9	  const badgeClass = `badge ${verdictClass(verdict)}`;
10	
11	  return (
12	    <div className="card">
13	      <h2>
14	        Screen
15	        <span className={badgeClass}>{verdict ?? 'UNKNOWN'}</span>
16	        {profit_stage && <span style={{ color: '#777', fontWeight: 400, fontSize: '0.85rem' }}>
17	          {profit_stage}
18	        </span>}
19	      </h2>
20	
21	      <div className="ratios">
22	        {Object.entries(ratios).map(([key, value]) => (
23	          <div key={key} className="ratio">
24	            <div className="label">{ratioLabel(key)}</div>
25	            <div className="value">{formatRatio(value)}</div>
26	          </div>
27	        ))}
28	      </div>
29	
30	      {rationale && <p className="rationale">{rationale}</p>}
31	    </div>
32	  );
33	}
34	
```

1	import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
2	import { loadReports } from './lib/reports.js';
3	import ScreenReport from './components/ScreenReport.jsx';
4	
5	const TABS = ['Screen', 'Signal', 'Model', 'Timing', 'Summary'];
6	
7	export default function App() {
8	  const reports = useMemo(() => loadReports(), []);
9	  const [selectedPath, setSelectedPath] = useState(reports[0]?.path ?? null);
10	  const [tab, setTab] = useState('Screen');
11	
12	  const current = reports.find((r) => r.path === selectedPath) ?? reports[0];
13	
14	  if (!current) {
15	    return (
16	      <div className="app">
17	        <h1>Equity Research</h1>
18	        <p className="empty">No reports found in <code>reports/</code>.</p>
19	      </div>
20	    );
21	  }
22	
23	  const { data } = current;
24	  const stages = data.stages ?? {};
25	
26	  return (
27	    <div className="app">
28	      <div className="header">
29	        <h1>{data.ticker}{data.company ? ` — ${data.company}` : ''}</h1>
30	        <span className="date">{data.date}</span>
31	        <select
32	          className="picker"
33	          name="report"
34	          aria-label="Select report"
35	          value={current.path}
36	          onChange={(e) => setSelectedPath(e.target.value)}
37	        >
38	          {reports.map((r) => (
39	            <option key={r.path} value={r.path}>
40	              {r.ticker} — {r.dateStr}
41	            </option>
42	          ))}
43	        </select>
44	      </div>
45	
46	      <div className="tabs">
47	        {TABS.map((name) => {
48	          const key = name.toLowerCase();
49	          const hasData = key === 'summary' ? true : Boolean(stages[key]);
50	          return (
51	            <button
52	              key={name}
53	              className={`tab ${tab === name ? 'active' : ''}`}
54	              onClick={() => setTab(name)}
55	              disabled={!hasData}
56	            >
57	              {name}
58	            </button>
59	          );
60	        })}
61	      </div>
62	
63	      {tab === 'Screen' && <ScreenReport stage={stages.screen} />}
64	      {tab !== 'Screen' && <p className="empty">{tab} view not yet implemented.</p>}
65	    </div>
66	  );
67	}
68

```
1	export function verdictClass(verdict) {
2	  switch ((verdict || '').toUpperCase()) {
3	    case 'PASS':
4	    case 'BUY':
5	    case 'ACT NOW':
6	      return 'pass';
7	    case 'WATCH':
8	    case 'CONDITIONAL':
9	    case 'WAIT FOR CATALYST':
10	      return 'watch';
11	    case 'SKIP':
12	    case 'CAUTION':
13	    case 'WAIT FOR BETTER ENTRY':
14	      return 'skip';
15	    default:
16	      return 'unknown';
17	  }
18	}
19	
20	const RATIO_LABELS = {
21	  pe_ratio: 'P/E',
22	  ps_ratio: 'P/S',
23	  ev_ebitda: 'EV/EBITDA',
24	  pfcf: 'P/FCF',
25	  ev_revenue: 'EV/Revenue'
26	};
27	
28	export function ratioLabel(key) {
29	  return RATIO_LABELS[key] ?? key;
30	}
31	
32	export function formatRatio(value) {
33	  if (value == null || Number.isNaN(value)) return '—';
34	  return Number(value).toFixed(1);
35	}
36	
```

> AGENT

Key observations from the four reports:

- **AMZN** is ESTABLISHED two-stage DCF (full new schema with `growth_rate`, `fcf_margin_ttm_reported`, `gate_bypass`).
- **META** is ESTABLISHED two-stage DCF but older — no `growth_rate` block, no reported margin.
- **NVDA** is `method: "pre-profit (...)"` despite `profit_stage: "ESTABLISHED"` → hits the pre-profit branch.
- **RDDT** is pre-profit.

So the four-ticker cycle naturally tests both branches. `range_vs_price` values seen in the wild: `MARGIN OF SAFETY`, `WITHIN BEAR-BASE`, `WITHIN BASE-BULL` — none of these are in `verdictClass` today, so I'll extend it.

**Assumptions for this slice (correct me if wrong):**
1. Branch on `stage.method?.startsWith('pre-profit')` — not on `profit_stage` (NVDA proves they're independent).
2. `range_vs_price` colour mapping: `MARGIN OF SAFETY` / `WITHIN BEAR-BASE` → pass (green); `WITHIN BASE-BULL` → watch (amber); `ABOVE BULL` → skip (red).
3. Header row for both branches: method, profit_stage, base WACC, NTM revenue, clean FCF margin TTM (all four target reports have these).
4. Position-sizing rationale (from `stage.position_sizing.rationale`) is the one-liner — no top-level `rationale` exists on the model stage.
5. Skip AMZN's `fcf_margin_normalized` / `normalization_note` in the shell — Slice 5 surfaces that.

Proceeding.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/anton/src/stock-review/ui/src/lib/formatters.js",
  "old_string": "export function formatRatio(value) {\n  if (value == null || Number.isNaN(value)) return '—';\n  return Number(value).toFixed(1);\n}",
  "new_string": "export function formatRatio(value) {\n  if (value == null || Number.isNaN(value)) return '—';\n  return Number(value).toFixed(1);\n}\n\nfunction isNum(value) {\n  return value != null && !Number.isNaN(Number(value));\n}\n\nexport function formatCurrency(value) {\n  if (!isNum(value)) return '—';\n  const n = Number(value);\n  if (Math.abs(n) >= 1000) return `$${n.toFixed(0)}`;\n  return `$${n.toFixed(2)}`;\n}\n\nexport function formatPercent(value, digits = 1) {\n  if (!isNum(value)) return '—';\n  return `${(Number(value) * 100).toFixed(digits)}%`;\n}\n\nexport function formatBillions(value) {\n  if (!isNum(value)) return '—';\n  const billions = Number(value) / 1e9;\n  if (Math.abs(billions) >= 100) return `$${billions.toFixed(0)}B`;\n  return `$${billions.toFixed(1)}B`;\n}\n\nexport function formatPerShare(value) {\n  if (!isNum(value)) return '—';\n  return `$${Number(value).toFixed(2)}/sh`;\n}"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/anton/src/stock-review/ui/src/components/ModelReport.jsx",
  "content": "import {\n  verdictClass,\n  formatCurrency,\n  formatPercent,\n  formatBillions\n} from '../lib/formatters.js';\n\nconst PRE_PROFIT_NOTE = 'Visual breakdown is ESTABLISHED-only for v1.';\n\nfunction HeaderCell({ label, value }) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"model-header-cell\">\n      <div className=\"label\">{label}</div>\n      <div className=\"value\">{value}</div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction IvCell({ label, value, accent }) {\n  return (\n    <div className={`iv-cell${accent ? ` iv-cell-${accent}` : ''}`}>\n      <div className=\"label\">{label}</div>\n      <div className=\"value\">{formatCurrency(value)}</div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function ModelReport({ stage }) {\n  if (!stage) {\n    return <p className=\"empty\">No model data in this report.</p>;\n  }\n\n  const {\n    method,\n    profit_stage,\n    base_wacc,\n    ntm_revenue,\n    fcf_margin_ttm,\n    current_price,\n    intrinsic_value_range = {},\n    range_vs_price,\n    position_sizing = {}\n  } = stage;\n\n  const isPreProfit = typeof method === 'string' && method.startsWith('pre-profit');\n  const rangeClass = `badge ${verdictClass(range_vs_price)}`;\n  const signalVerdict = position_sizing.signal_verdict;\n  const signalClass = signalVerdict ? `badge ${verdictClass(signalVerdict)}` : null;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>\n        Model\n        {range_vs_price && <span className={rangeClass}>{range_vs_price}</span>}\n        {profit_stage && (\n          <span style={{ color: '#777', fontWeight: 400, fontSize: '0.85rem' }}>\n            {profit_stage}\n          </span>\n        )}\n      </h2>\n\n      <div className=\"model-header\">\n        <HeaderCell label=\"Method\" value={method ?? '—'} />\n        <HeaderCell label=\"Base WACC\" value={formatPercent(base_wacc)} />\n        <HeaderCell label=\"NTM Revenue\" value={formatBillions(ntm_revenue)} />\n        <HeaderCell label=\"Clean FCF Margin TTM\" value={formatPercent(fcf_margin_ttm)} />\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"iv-range\">\n        <IvCell label=\"Bear\" value={intrinsic_value_range.bear} accent=\"bear\" />\n        <IvCell label=\"Base\" value={intrinsic_value_range.base} accent=\"base\" />\n        <IvCell label=\"Bull\" value={intrinsic_value_range.bull} accent=\"bull\" />\n        <IvCell label=\"Current\" value={current_price} accent=\"current\" />\n      </div>\n\n      {position_sizing.band && (\n        <div className=\"position-sizing\">\n          <div className=\"position-sizing-line\">\n            <span className=\"label\">Position sizing</span>\n            <span className=\"band\">{position_sizing.band}</span>\n            {signalVerdict && <span className={signalClass}>{signalVerdict}</span>}\n          </div>\n          {position_sizing.rationale && (\n            <p className=\"rationale\">{position_sizing.rationale}</p>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {isPreProfit && <p className=\"empty pre-profit-note\">{PRE_PROFIT_NOTE}</p>}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": ".picker {\n  margin-left: auto;\n  padding: 0.35rem 0.5rem;\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n}\n\n.model-header {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(160px, 1fr));\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n  margin-top: 1rem;\n}\n\n.model-header-cell {\n  background: #fafafa;\n  border: 1px solid #eee;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.75rem;\n}\n\n.model-header-cell .label {\n  color: #777;\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.05em;\n}\n\n.model-header-cell .value {\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  margin-top: 0.25rem;\n  word-break: break-word;\n}\n\n.iv-range {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  margin-top: 1.25rem;\n}\n\n.iv-cell {\n  background: #fafafa;\n  border: 1px solid #e5e5e5;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  padding: 0.6rem 0.75rem;\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n.iv-cell .label {\n  color: #777;\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.05em;\n}\n\n.iv-cell .value {\n  font-size: 1.15rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  margin-top: 0.25rem;\n}\n\n.iv-cell-current {\n  border-color: #1a1a1a;\n  background: #fff;\n}\n\n.iv-cell-current .label {\n  color: #1a1a1a;\n}\n\n.position-sizing {\n  margin-top: 1.25rem;\n  padding-top: 1rem;\n  border-top: 1px solid #eee;\n}\n\n.position-sizing-line {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n}\n\n.position-sizing-line .label {\n  color: #777;\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.05em;\n}\n\n.position-sizing-line .band {\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-size: 1rem;\n}\n\n.pre-profit-note {\n  margin-top: 1rem;\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n}"
}

1	const modules = import.meta.glob('../../../reports/*.json', { eager: true });

3	function parseFilename(path) {
4	  const file = path.split('/').pop().replace(/\.json$/, '');
5	  const [ticker, dateStr] = file.split('_');
6	  return { ticker, dateStr };
7	}
8	
9	export function loadReports() {
10	  const reports = Object.entries(modules).map(([path, mod]) => {
11	    const { ticker, dateStr } = parseFilename(path);
12	    return { path, ticker, dateStr, data: mod.default ?? mod };
13	  });
14	  reports.sort((a, b) => b.dateStr.localeCompare(a.dateStr) || b.ticker.localeCompare(a.ticker));
15	  return reports;
16	}
17

<issue identifier="ABA-136">
<title>[2/7] Extend Model SKILL — historical_fcf_margins[] (v1.11)</title>
<description>
**Parent:** <issue id="5ae08460-bbc6-42f9-94b3-14105498e30e">ABA-115</issue>
**Plan:** `tasks/plan.md` → Slice 2
**Skill to use:** `/agent-skills:build` (no UI lens needed — single SKILL.md edit)
**Depends on:** \[1/7\] passing its gate (don't touch SKILL until shell is verified rendering)

## Goal

Extend `/stock-model` to emit `stages.model.historical_fcf_margins[]` — a 3y per-year series of clean and reported FCF margins. The UI's margin-trajectory chart (Slice 5) needs this; current JSON only has TTM scalars.

## Files to touch

`skills/stock-model/SKILL.md` only. Four insertion points (line numbers may drift — confirm via grep):

1. **COMPUTE Step 1 (\~line 316)** — after `fcf_cagr_3y` derivation. For each `y ∈ [0..3]` with non-null `revenue` and `stock_based_compensation`, compute:
   * `fcf_margin_clean = (years[y].free_cash_flow − years[y].stock_based_compensation) / years[y].revenue`
   * `fcf_margin_reported = years[y].free_cash_flow / years[y].revenue`
     Persist as `historical_fcf_margins`, newest-first, matching `years[]` order. Omit years missing required fields — keep the array dense.
2. **ESTABLISHED JSON schema block (\~line 567)** — add `"historical_fcf_margins": [{"year": "YYYY-MM-DD", "fcf_margin_clean": <number>, "fcf_margin_reported": <number>}, ...]` immediately after `fcf_margin_ttm_reported`, before `fcf_cagr_3y`.
3. **Pre-profit JSON schema block (\~line 875)** — emit the same field when computable. UI does not render it for pre-profit in v1, but the audit trail is the same value and worth \~5 lines.
4. **Printed OUTPUT block (\~line 512)** — add audit row:
   `Historical clean FCF margin: y-3 X.X% / y-2 X.X% / y-1 X.X% / TTM X.X%`
   so the JSON value is human-verifiable against the same run.
5. **Changelog (\~line 1101+, convention** `### v1.N — ABA-XXX`**)** — add `### v1.11 — ABA-115 (historical FCF margin series for Model-tab visuals)` with 3–5 acceptance bullets.

## Acceptance criteria

* Field documented in both ESTABLISHED and pre-profit JSON OUTPUT schema blocks.
* Printed audit row reconciles cell-for-cell with the new JSON array.
* Changelog entry committed in the same diff.

## Verification

Smoke-run one ESTABLISHED ticker:

```
/stock-signal META
/stock-model META
```

Open the printed OUTPUT block; confirm the audit row's four values match the resulting `reports/META_YYYYMMDD.json` `historical_fcf_margins[]` values rounded to the same precision.

## Gate to Slice 3 (\[3/7\])

**Stop** if audit-row and JSON values disagree — either the COMPUTE step or the OUTPUT print step is wrong; debug before mass re-runs. Cell-for-cell agreement is the gate.

<id>5ae08460-bbc6-42f9-94b3-14105498e30e</id>
<title>/stock:model report — glidepath + scenario fan visuals</title>
<description>
## Problem

After <issue id="89e1f4f4-8b04-4c5b-9931-7f4f837de45f">ABA-110</issue> (SBC (Stock-Based Compensation) strip) and <issue id="e78e36cd-49f1-441f-96a9-317acc594230">ABA-111</issue> (growth-rate cap) landed, `/stock:model` now produces honest but **stark-looking** scenario outputs. Example META 2026-05-17:

* Bear IV (Intrinsic Value): $184
* Base IV: $393
* Bull IV: $778
* Current price: $614

Reading the base case in isolation ("$393 vs $614 → 36% overvalued") is misleading without the trajectory context. The base case mechanically applies the consensus growth ceiling in Y2 (META: 52% trailing → 18% capped), which looks like a cliff but is a smoothing artifact of the spec. A reader anchored on "base = realistic central case" will misread it as "the model thinks META is 36% overvalued" when the actual message is "the market is pricing META as a bull case — here's what has to keep going right."

## Why now

Thesis construction depends on **explaining how the numbers got where they got** — more than the numbers themselves. Without visuals, the reader has to do the unpacking in their head; with visuals, the trajectory and the asymmetry are immediately legible.

User quote (2026-05-17 session): *"reading a 'base case' that's so far from the current stock price has some context — it's unlikely that a company that has kept massive momentum in growth will suddenly stop, so this is important to put into context."*

## Scope

Add three visuals to the report UI for ESTABLISHED-path Model output:

1. **CAGR (Compound Annual Growth Rate) glidepath** — line chart showing trailing-3y CAGR (clean, SBC-stripped), then Y1→Y5 projected CAGR for each scenario (bear/base/bull). Annotate cap point ("capped from 52.1% by 18% fallback ceiling" for the base line).
2. **FCF (Free Cash Flow) margin trajectory** — area chart showing 3y historical reported vs clean FCF margin, with a constant-margin extension for Y1→Y5. Visually surfaces that base case assumes flat-line margins (no operating leverage) — and invites the user to consider whether they buy that assumption.
3. **Scenario fan chart** — cone/fan plot of Y1→Y5 FCF projection for bear/base/bull, with the corresponding terminal-year discounted-to-PV (Present Value) markers, and the current price line for reference.

## Acceptance criteria

1. UI renders the three visuals for any covered ticker's latest `reports/TICKER_YYYYMMDD.json` where `stages.model` is present.
2. Cap-point annotation reads from `stages.model.growth_rate` block (added in <issue id="e78e36cd-49f1-441f-96a9-317acc594230">ABA-111</issue>) — specifically `trailing_3y_cagr`, `applied_base_cagr`, `cap_source`.
3. FCF margin chart reads clean and reported series from `stages.model.fcf_margin_ttm` (clean) and `stages.model.fcf_margin_ttm_reported` (reported, added in <issue id="89e1f4f4-8b04-4c5b-9931-7f4f837de45f">ABA-110</issue>).
4. Scenario fan chart sources from `stages.model.scenarios.{bear,base,bull}` (existing).
5. Each visual has a one-sentence caption explaining the assumption it surfaces (not the chart mechanics — the *thesis* implication).
6. Pre-profit variant either gets its own analogous visuals or is explicitly scoped out (decide during design).

## Out of scope

* Glidepath shaping (introducing year-by-year CAGR taper instead of flat Y2-Y5) — separate ticket if worth doing
* Adding a margin-expansion lever to *base* case — separate methodology ticket if worth doing
* Sensitivity heatmap (WACC × g) — separate ticket

## Related

* Builds on <issue id="89e1f4f4-8b04-4c5b-9931-7f4f837de45f">ABA-110</issue> (SBC strip surfaces clean vs reported margins in JSON)
* Builds on <issue id="e78e36cd-49f1-441f-96a9-317acc594230">ABA-111</issue> (growth-rate block surfaces cap mechanics in JSON)
* Likely lives in Wave 4 (UI/report polish) of M5; defer until playbook layer (<issue id="70664e55-f843-4fed-8462-d8e6e78141cb">ABA-112</issue>) lands so visuals reference playbook narrative

## Methodology footnote (for the reader)

The base case is intentionally a **"stop overdelivering"** anchor:

* Margins flat at today's clean FCF margin (no operating-leverage assumption)
* Growth decelerates from trailing print to consensus-5y ceiling (or 18% fallback) in Y2
* Discount rate at WACC (Weighted Average Cost of Capital) baseline

The bull case is where margin-expansion + sustained-growth assumptions live. This is by design — it forces the thesis to articulate which levers it's betting on, rather than baking optimism into the central case.
</description>

</issue>

300	
301	When `status = "applied"`, COMPUTE Step 2 (Year-1 FCF anchor) uses `base_anchor_multiplier` from this sub-section on the **base** scenario only. The output cap (Step 6b in COMPUTE) may further clamp the multiplier; `clamped_from` records the pre-clamp value. When `status ∈ {unavailable, no_kpi_mapping, user_skipped, direction_disagreement}`, `base_anchor_multiplier = 1.00` and COMPUTE Step 2b is a no-op.
302	
303	### COMPUTE — Two-stage DCF
304	
305	Execute in order. Show the working — every scenario must be reproducible from the printed assumptions.
306	
307	**Step 1 — Derive trailing FCF base and margin (SBC-stripped per ABA-110).**
308	
309	yfinance's OCF adds SBC back as a non-cash charge, so reported `free_cash_flow` silently treats stock-based compensation as free. `/stock-signal` already strips SBC from EPS (Step 5); the DCF base must do the same or the two skills disagree on the same company. Strip first, then derive margin and CAGR.
310	
311	- `sbc_ttm` = `years[0].stock_based_compensation` (refuse via the GATHER null-handler above if null — never substitute, never assume zero).
312	- `fcf_ttm_reported` = `years[0].free_cash_flow` (latest reported FY; if quarterly TTM is unavailable, accept the latest annual figure). Held for the audit trail and the OUTPUT block; **not** used as the DCF anchor.
313	- `fcf_ttm` = `fcf_ttm_reported − sbc_ttm` (the SBC-stripped, "clean" FCF — this is the DCF base).
314	- `fcf_margin_ttm_reported` = `fcf_ttm_reported / years[0].revenue` (audit-trail only; surfaces in OUTPUT side-by-side with the clean margin).
315	- `fcf_margin_ttm` = `fcf_ttm / years[0].revenue` (the **clean** SBC-stripped margin — this is the margin that anchors the Y1 FCF projection in Step 2).
316	- `fcf_cagr_3y` is computed on **clean** FCF on both ends — never on reported. For each historical year used, derive `clean_fcf_y = years[y].free_cash_flow − years[y].stock_based_compensation`. Then `fcf_cagr_3y = (clean_fcf_y0 / clean_fcf_y3) ^ (1/3) − 1` when 4y of usable history is available; if only 3y is available use `^(1/2)` with the oldest available year as the base; if only 2y is available, set `fcf_cagr_3y = (clean_fcf_y0 / clean_fcf_yprior) − 1` and flag confidence = MEDIUM. State which formulation was used. If any historical-year `stock_based_compensation` is null, the SBC paste-in (GATHER null-handler) must cover the full required series — never silently fall back to reported-FCF CAGR for the missing year.
317	
318	**Step 2 — Year-1 FCF anchor under each scenario.**
319	
320	Year-1 FCF is anchored on NTM consensus, then perturbed per scenario.
321	
322	Determine the FCF margin anchor:
323	- When playbook provides `terminal_margin`: `fcf_margin_anchor = playbook.terminal_margin` (normalised steady-state margin; replaces TTM when capex cycles or one-time costs distort the trailing window). Surface in OUTPUT as `FCF margin anchor: X.X% (playbook override; TTM clean: Y.Y%)`.
324	- Otherwise: `fcf_margin_anchor = fcf_margin_ttm` (SBC-stripped TTM margin from Step 1).
325	
326	Compute `fcf_y1_consensus = ntm_revenue × fcf_margin_anchor` as the consensus anchor.
327	
328	| Scenario | Y1 FCF |
329	|---|---|
330	| Bear | `fcf_y1_consensus × 0.85` — revenue miss + 200 bps margin compression |
331	| Base | `fcf_y1_consensus × base_anchor_multiplier` — NTM consensus, perturbed by the engagement modifier (see Step 2b) |
332	| Bull | `fcf_y1_consensus × 1.10` — revenue beat + operating leverage |
333	
334	**Step 2b — Engagement modifier on base scenario (FR3/FR4, C3/C4/C5).**
335	
336	When the engagement-modifier GATHER sub-step emitted `status: "applied"`, the base scenario's Y1 FCF anchor is perturbed by `base_anchor_multiplier ∈ [0.96, 1.04]` from that sub-step. When `status ∈ {unavailable, no_kpi_mapping, user_skipped, direction_disagreement}` OR when the `--engagement-modifier` flag was absent, `base_anchor_multiplier = 1.00` and this step is a no-op (the base scenario's Y1 anchor equals `fcf_y1_consensus` unchanged).
337	
338	Bear and bull are **never** modifier-perturbed — scenario-axis independence (C5) and the range-integrity invariant `bear_iv < base_iv < bull_iv` both depend on the modifier touching base alone.
339	
340	**Output cap (C4 / FR4b / NFR4 — applied AFTER Step 6 produces base IV.)** Per the ADR, the modifier's effect on the base scenario's per-share intrinsic value is additionally capped at ≤5%. This cap is evaluated post-COMPUTE in **Step 6b** below (after the equity bridge produces `intrinsic_value_per_share`), because the input multiplier alone cannot predict the IV impact — DCF leverage means a 4% Y1 nudge can translate to anywhere from 2% to 9% on per-share IV depending on the WACC / terminal-growth regime.
341	
342	**Revision-direction agreement guardrail** (spike-decision §"Lead vs confirm", wired in GATHER Step 5b). When engagement direction and the trailing 30-day EPS revision direction strictly disagree, GATHER emits `status: "direction_disagreement"` with `base_anchor_multiplier = 1.00`, and this step is a no-op. When they agree (or revision data is unavailable — legacy fall-through), the modifier applies per C3/C4 and the audit trail records `agreement: true | null` respectively.
343	
344	**Step 3 — Years 2–5 FCF growth per scenario (sanity-capped per ABA-111).**
345	
346	Trailing 3y CAGR is a useful **floor** signal (the company actually grew this fast recently) but a poor **central case** when the trailing window includes a depressed base year — extrapolating that rate produces mechanical, not analytical, terminal FCFs (META FY22 was the Reality Labs / cost-reset trough; NVDA FY23 pre-dated the AI boom). Cap base-scenario growth against a consensus or fallback ceiling, then drive bear/bull off the capped base so scenario-axis independence is preserved.
347	
348	```
349	consensus_growth = get_estimates(ticker).eps_growth_5y     # long-term sell-side consensus; may be null
350	fallback_ceiling = 0.18                                     # 18% — historical mature-tech ceiling
351	playbook_ceiling = playbook.growth_ceiling if playbook.loaded else None   # ABA-112: playbook override
352	
353	# Priority: consensus (most current external data) > playbook ceiling > generic fallback
354	ceiling          = (consensus_growth if consensus_growth is not None
355	                   else (playbook_ceiling if playbook_ceiling is not None
356	                   else fallback_ceiling))
357	base_cagr        = min(fcf_cagr_3y, ceiling)
358	cap_applied      = (base_cagr < fcf_cagr_3y)
359	cap_source       = ("consensus_5y_growth"   if (consensus_growth is not None and cap_applied)
360	                   else "playbook"          if (playbook_ceiling is not None and cap_applied)
361	                   else "fallback_ceiling"  if cap_applied
362	                   else None)
363	```
364	
365	The cap only narrows growth — it never raises it. If `fcf_cagr_3y` is already at or below the ceiling, `base_cagr = fcf_cagr_3y` and `cap_applied = false` (e.g. GOOG, whose trailing FCF CAGR runs near consensus — the cap does not fire and the output discloses that explicitly: `Y2-5 CAGR: X% (trailing 3y, within consensus ceiling)`).
366	
367	Bear and bull formulas operate on the **capped** `base_cagr`, not on the raw trailing CAGR. This preserves bear/base/bull axis independence (each scenario applies its own multiplier on a common anchor) while preventing the entire range from inheriting a trough-extrapolation.
368	
369	| Scenario | Y2–Y5 CAGR | Narrative |
370	|---|---|---|
371	| Bear | `max(base_cagr × 0.50, 0.02)` | Secular deceleration; growth halves and floors at GDP |
372	| Base | `base_cagr` (capped per above) | Trailing 3y or consensus/fallback ceiling, whichever is lower |
373	| Bull | `base_cagr × 1.20 + 0.02` | Operating leverage extends runway; 200 bps uplift on the capped base |
374	
375	Project: `fcf_y_n = fcf_y_(n−1) × (1 + cagr)` for n = 2, 3, 4, 5.
376	
377	When `cap_applied = true`, the OUTPUT block's Base scenario line must surface the cap explicitly (`Y2-5 CAGR: X.X% (capped from trailing Y.Y% by <cap_source>)`); silent caps are forbidden. The `growth_rate` JSON block (see OUTPUT) records all five fields (trailing rate, consensus value, fallback ceiling, applied rate, cap source) every run — even when the cap does not fire — so the audit trail is symmetric.
378	
379	**Step 4 — Terminal value (Gordon Growth) per scenario.**
380	
381	| Scenario | Terminal growth `g` | WACC | Narrative |
382	|---|---|---|---|
383	| Bear | 1.5% | `base_wacc + 1.0pp` | Recession tail; cost of capital widens |
384	| Base | 2.5% | `base_wacc` | Long-run growth normalises to GDP+; current rates |
385	| Bull | 3.5% | `base_wacc − 1.0pp` | Above-GDP terminal; rate-easing tailwind |
386	
387	Terminal value at end of Year 5: `TV = fcf_y5 × (1 + g) / (wacc − g)`. Require `wacc > g` — if violated (sensitivity edge), narrow the WACC adjustment for that scenario and state the override in the output.
388	
389	**Step 5 — Discount to present.**
```

652	
653	When no playbook is present: `"playbook": {"loaded": false, "path": null, "last_updated": null, "overrides_applied": [], "failure_modes": []}`.
654	
655	`overrides_applied` lists exactly the frontmatter fields that were consumed (i.e., had a non-null value and modified a DCF input or output). It does not list fields that were present but had no effect (e.g. `failure_modes` that were only displayed, or `last_updated`). Always emitted — even when `loaded: false` — so replay diffing can detect a missing playbook.
656	
657	The `engagement_modifier` block is emitted only when `--engagement-modifier` was passed. When the flag is absent, omit the block entirely (FR6 / spike-decision). When the flag is present but the modifier was not applied (any non-`applied` status), populate `status` and `status_reason` and emit the remaining fields as `null` — preserving the schema shape for replay diffing. When `status == "applied"`, all populated fields are required (NFR3). `kpi_map_schema_version` mirrors the top-level field of `skills/_shared/engagement_kpi_map.json` at run time per Task 8 ADR D5. `clamped_from` is non-null only when the output-cap clamp fired (Step 6b); otherwise `null`. `revision` and `agreement` are populated by GATHER Step 5b (Yahoo `/analysis/` EPS Trend fetch); when the fetch fails or parsing returns no values, `revision.revision_pct` and `revision.direction` are `null` and `agreement` is `null` (legacy fall-through — modifier still applies per C3/C4). `agreement == false` triggers the `direction_disagreement` status, in which case `base_anchor_multiplier = 1.00` (modifier suppressed per spike-decision guardrail).
658	
659	`fcf_ttm`, `fcf_margin_ttm`, and `fcf_cagr_3y` are the **SBC-stripped (clean)** values per ABA-110 — they anchor the DCF. `fcf_ttm_reported`, `fcf_margin_ttm_reported`, and `sbc_ttm` are audit-only fields exposing the pre-strip numbers so consumers can reconcile against yfinance's `free_cash_flow` and `stock_based_compensation` directly. The two pairs must satisfy `fcf_ttm == fcf_ttm_reported − sbc_ttm` and `fcf_margin_ttm == fcf_ttm / revenue_ttm` (within float tolerance).
660	
661	`growth_rate` is emitted every run per ABA-111. `trailing_3y_cagr` mirrors `fcf_cagr_3y` (the raw clean-FCF trailing CAGR, pre-cap). `consensus_5y_growth` is the value pulled from `get_estimates(ticker).eps_growth_5y` — `null` when yfinance has no long-term consensus. `fallback_ceiling` is the hard-coded 18% ceiling used when consensus is null. `applied_base_cagr` is `min(trailing_3y_cagr, ceiling)` — the value that drove the base scenario's Y2–Y5 projection (and the anchor that bear/bull scale off). `cap_applied` is `true` when `applied_base_cagr < trailing_3y_cagr`; `cap_source` is `consensus_5y_growth` / `fallback_ceiling` / `null` respectively. When the cap does not fire (trailing already at or below ceiling), `applied_base_cagr == trailing_3y_cagr` and `cap_source == null`.
662	
663	`intrinsic_value_range` is a flat extract of `scenarios.{bear,base,bull}.intrinsic_value_per_share` for UI convenience — must reconcile cell-for-cell with the scenarios block.
664	
665	Rows in `sensitivity_table.intrinsic_value_per_share` are indexed by `wacc_axis` (row 0 = lowest WACC); columns by `terminal_growth_axis` (col 0 = lowest g). `base_cell` names the index that should match `scenarios.base.intrinsic_value_per_share`. Suppressed cells (where `wacc ≤ g`) are emitted as `null`, not numbers.
666

672	```
673	
674	**Confidence rules (`meta.confidence`):**
675	
676	- HIGH: 4y of FCF history, SBC retrieved from MCP for every year used (no SBC paste-in), no manual inputs other than base WACC, scenario range check passed first time.
677	- MEDIUM: 2–3y of FCF history, or any of net_debt / shares paste-in fired, **or SBC paste-in fired for any year used in the DCF base or CAGR (per ABA-110)**, or range integrity check required user re-input.
678	- LOW: <2y FCF history (should not reach COMPUTE — VALIDATE refuses earlier), or `wacc > g` constraint required mid-scenario override.
679	
680	The SBC paste-in cap is one-way and unconditional: if `years[].stock_based_compensation` for any year used in `fcf_ttm` or `fcf_cagr_3y` was sourced via Manual Input Protocol (`source: "user_paste"`), `meta.confidence` caps at MEDIUM regardless of other inputs. SBC is methodologically load-bearing — Signal and Model both depend on the same TTM SBC figure for consistency (acceptance criterion 5 on ABA-110), and a pasted figure has weaker provenance than a structured MCP fetch.
681	
682	Take the min of any prior stage's confidence and the model's own confidence — never upgrade prior-stage confidence here.
683

687	
688	Run these MCP calls in this order. Every retrieved figure is held with its raw value; never round during gather.

700	**Manual Input Protocol fires (always grouped — one paste-in):**
701	
702	- **Base WACC** — same rule as ESTABLISHED; yfinance has no risk-free-rate / beta surface. Never default.
703	- **Exit EV/Revenue multiple — base case.** Present the comp list and ask the user to confirm or override the base multiple. Bear is `base × 0.6`, bull is `base × 1.4` (state these scenario axes; do not ask separately).
704	- **Terminal clean FCF margin target.** Industry-anchored, **SBC-stripped** (per ABA-110). When playbook provides `terminal_margin`, present it as a pre-filled value — `Terminal clean FCF margin: X.X% (from playbook — confirm or override:)`. Otherwise ask the user for the steady-state *clean* FCF margin the company is expected to achieve by Year 5 under the base case — i.e. FCF after stock-based compensation has been deducted, not the industry-reported number. State the distinction explicitly in the prompt so users do not anchor on SBC-included margins. Bear is `target − 5 pp`, bull is `target + 5 pp`. Never default.
705	- **SBC dilution rate (annual %).** Pre-fill with the trailing 3y dilution rate derived from `shares_outstanding_diluted` history: `dilution_3y = (years[0].shares / years[3].shares) ^ (1/3) − 1`. Surface this number and the historical share-count series, and ask the user to confirm or override. Bear is `confirmed × 1.5`, bull is `confirmed × 0.5` (dilution accelerates in bear, decelerates in bull) — state these axes; do not ask separately.
706	
707	Manual inputs are tagged `source: "user_paste"`; `meta.confidence` caps at MEDIUM. If the user accepts every pre-filled value with no override, still record `source: "user_confirmed"` — the value is load-bearing on the output.
708	
709	If `get_financials` returns null `stock_based_compensation` for the latest year: refuse with `Pre-profit variant requires SBC data; yf MCP returned null. Re-run with --confirm only after SBC is verified manually.` Do not proceed without SBC — the dilution schedule is a first-class output.
710	
711	### COMPUTE — Pre-profit (revenue-multiple exit + FCF inflection)
712	
713	Execute in order. The math anchors on revenue (not FCF) because pre-profit companies do not yet have a stable FCF base.
714	
715	**Step 1 — Revenue trajectory per scenario (sanity-capped per ABA-111).**
716	
717	- `rev_cagr_3y = (years[0].revenue / years[3].revenue) ^ (1/3) − 1` (use shorter history with the same degraded formulations as ESTABLISHED Step 1 if 4y is unavailable).
718	- Apply the same growth-ceiling pattern as ESTABLISHED Step 3, with a higher fallback because pre-profit tech routinely grows revenue 30–40% sustainably:
719	
720	  ```
721	  consensus_rev_growth = derived from get_estimates if available  # currently null on yf — placeholder for when an EDGAR/sell-side revenue-growth estimate lands
722	  fallback_ceiling     = 0.35                                      # 35% — pre-profit revenue ceiling
723	  playbook_ceiling     = playbook.growth_ceiling if playbook.loaded else None   # ABA-112: playbook override
724	
725	  # Priority: consensus > playbook ceiling > generic fallback
726	  ceiling              = (consensus_rev_growth if consensus_rev_growth is not None
727	                         else (playbook_ceiling if playbook_ceiling is not None
728	                         else fallback_ceiling))
729	  base_rev_cagr        = min(rev_cagr_3y, ceiling)
730	  rev_cap_applied      = (base_rev_cagr < rev_cagr_3y)
731	  rev_cap_source       = ("consensus_rev_growth" if (consensus_rev_growth is not None and rev_cap_applied)
732	                         else "playbook"         if (playbook_ceiling is not None and rev_cap_applied)
733	                         else "fallback_ceiling" if rev_cap_applied
734	                         else None)
735	  ```
736	
737	- Year-1 revenue is anchored on NTM consensus, perturbed per scenario. Y2–Y5 CAGRs operate on the **capped** `base_rev_cagr`:
738	
739	| Scenario | Y1 revenue | Y2–Y5 CAGR |
740	|---|---|---|
741	| Bear | `ntm_revenue × 0.85` | `max(base_rev_cagr × 0.50, 0.05)` |
742	| Base | `ntm_revenue` | `base_rev_cagr` (capped) |
743	| Bull | `ntm_revenue × 1.15` | `base_rev_cagr × 1.15 + 0.03` |
744	
745	Project `revenue_y_n = revenue_y_(n−1) × (1 + cagr)` for n = 2..5. When `rev_cap_applied = true`, the OUTPUT block's Base scenario revenue-CAGR figure must surface the cap explicitly (same disclosure pattern as ESTABLISHED), and the `growth_rate` JSON block (see OUTPUT) records the five fields every run.
746	
747	**Step 2 — FCF margin trajectory and inflection year.**
748	
749	- `sbc_ttm` = `years[0].stock_based_compensation` (already required by the GATHER refusal above — non-null by the time this step runs).
750	- `fcf_ttm_reported = years[0].free_cash_flow` (audit-trail only).
751	- `fcf_ttm_clean = fcf_ttm_reported − sbc_ttm` (SBC-stripped FCF — the cash-flow side of the trajectory).
752	- `margin_ttm_reported = fcf_ttm_reported / years[0].revenue` (audit-trail only; surfaced in OUTPUT side-by-side with clean).
753	- `margin_ttm = fcf_ttm_clean / years[0].revenue` (the **clean** SBC-stripped margin — this is the trajectory baseline, may be negative). The user-confirmed terminal margin in the GATHER MIP is a **clean FCF margin target** — surface this label explicitly in the prompt so users do not anchor on industry-reported (SBC-included) margins.
754	- Target Year-5 margin per scenario: `target − 5pp` (bear) / `target` (base) / `target + 5pp` (bull), where `target` is the user-confirmed terminal clean FCF margin.
755	- Linear interpolation across Years 1–5: `margin_y_n = margin_ttm + (target − margin_ttm) × (n / 5)`.
756	- `fcf_y_n = revenue_y_n × margin_y_n` (negative in early years if the company is still cash-burning).
757	- **FCF inflection year:** the smallest n ∈ {1..5} such that `fcf_y_n ≥ 0` AND `fcf_y_m ≥ 0` for all m ≥ n in {1..5} (i.e., first year of *sustained* positive FCF — not a one-off blip).
758	- If no scenario reaches positive FCF by Y5, name it explicitly: `inflection_year = "beyond Y5"`. Do **not** silently assume eventual inflection — surface it as a model limitation in the OUTPUT block.
759	
760	**Step 3 — Exit enterprise value.**
761	
762	| Scenario | Exit EV/Revenue multiple | Exit EV at end of Year 5 |
763	|---|---|---|
764	| Bear | `base_multiple × 0.6` | `revenue_y5 × bear_multiple` |
765	| Base | `base_multiple` (user-confirmed) | `revenue_y5 × base_multiple` |
766	| Bull | `base_multiple × 1.4` | `revenue_y5 × bull_multiple` |
767	
768	No Gordon-growth terminal — the exit-multiple is the terminal. State that explicitly in the OUTPUT block.
769	
770	**Step 4 — SBC dilution schedule.**
771	
772	- `dilution_rate` per scenario: `confirmed_rate × 1.5` (bear) / `confirmed_rate` (base) / `confirmed_rate × 0.5` (bull).
773	- `shares_y_n = shares_y_(n−1) × (1 + dilution_rate)` for n = 1..5, starting from `years[0].shares_outstanding_diluted`.
774	- The Year-5 diluted share count is the divisor for per-share IV — using today's share count overstates IV. This is the whole reason dilution is first-class.
775	- Also report `dilution_pct_5y = (shares_y5 / shares_y0) − 1` for the OUTPUT block.
776	
777	**Step 5 — Discount to present.**
778	
779	For each scenario:
780	
781	```
782	EV_today = Σ_{n=1..5} fcf_y_n / (1 + wacc)^n   +   exit_ev / (1 + wacc)^5
783	```
784	
785	The first sum includes negative FCFs in early years if the company is still burning cash — this is a real present-value drag and must be carried (do not floor at zero).
786	
787	**Step 6 — Equity bridge and per-share intrinsic value.**
788	
789	- `net_debt = years[0].total_debt − years[0].cash` (keep the sign; negative means net cash).
790	- `equity_value = EV_today − net_debt`
791	- `intrinsic_value_per_share = equity_value / shares_y5`  ← **Year-5 diluted shares**, not today's.
792	- `upside_pct = (intrinsic_value_per_share / currentPrice − 1) × 100`
793	
794	**Step 7 — Range integrity check.**
795	
796	Same rule as ESTABLISHED: require `bear_iv < base_iv < bull_iv`. If violated, stop, surface the three scenario input/output tables, and ask the user to widen the exit-multiple, margin-target, or dilution-rate deltas. Never silently re-order.
797	
798	**Step 8 — Sensitivity note.**
799	
```

1086	57. **Rationalisations table covers growth cap →** the "trailing CAGR is what the data shows — capping it is fabrication" rebuttal is present and refers to ABA-111. The null-consensus and fallback-tweak rationalisations are also covered.
1087	
1088	### v1.9 — ABA-112 (playbook loader; depth-over-breadth foundation)
1089	
1090	58. **Loader fires for watchlist tickers →** `/stock-model META` with `playbooks/META.md` committed emits `Playbook loaded: playbooks/META.md (last updated YYYY-MM-DD). Overrides applied: [list]` before any MCP calls. The list includes every frontmatter field that had a non-null value.
1091	59. **No-playbook path unchanged →** invoking `/stock-model TSLA` (no `playbooks/TSLA.md`) emits `No playbook for TSLA — using generic ESTABLISHED/EMERGING logic.` All pre-ABA-112 acceptance criteria continue to pass on tickers without a playbook.
1092	60. **Override hierarchy — WACC →** when playbook supplies `base_wacc`, the MIP WACC question is replaced by a confirmation prompt. User can confirm (source: "playbook") or override (source: "user_paste", confidence caps at MEDIUM). `--wacc=X.X` at the command line bypasses both playbook and MIP.
1093	61. **Override hierarchy — growth ceiling →** when playbook supplies `growth_ceiling`, the cap-source in `growth_rate.cap_source` reads `"playbook"` (not `"fallback_ceiling"`). When sell-side consensus is also available, consensus takes precedence and `cap_source` reads `"consensus_5y_growth"`.
1094	62. **Override hierarchy — narrative →** when playbook supplies `bear_narrative`, `base_narrative`, or `bull_narrative`, the corresponding scenario's `Narrative:` line in the OUTPUT block reads the playbook string verbatim. Generic fallback strings are used only for scenarios that the playbook does not supply.
1095	63. **Audit trail intact →** `stages.model.playbook.overrides_applied` lists every frontmatter field that modified a DCF input or output. `stages.model.playbook.loaded` is `true` when a playbook was present; `false` otherwise. The block is always emitted — even for non-playbook tickers — with `loaded: false` and empty `overrides_applied`.
1096	64. **Monitor section →** when playbook supplies `failure_modes`, the OUTPUT block includes a `Monitor:` line after Position sizing listing each failure mode. The same list appears in `stages.model.playbook.failure_modes`.
1097	65. **Confidence anchor →** when playbook sets `confidence_anchor: MEDIUM`, the run's `meta.confidence` is capped at MEDIUM even when all other inputs are MCP-sourced and would yield HIGH. Cap is one-way (never raises); binding over runtime-computed confidence.
1098	66. **Playbook validation →** a playbook with malformed YAML frontmatter halts the run with a clear error message before any MCP call. Unknown frontmatter fields emit a per-field warning and do not fail the load.
1099	67. **META seed smoke →** `playbooks/META.md` exists in the repository; running `/stock-signal META` then `/stock-model META` produces a MODEL OUTPUT block where the WACC source, growth ceiling cap source (when it fires), and scenario narratives all reflect the playbook values; `stages.model.playbook.loaded == true`; `overrides_applied` lists the active overrides.
1100	
1101	### v1.10 — ABA-132 (profile-keyed GARP thresholds; COVERAGE.md bypass removed)
1102	
1103	68. **COVERAGE.md bypass removed →** the v1.9 bypass (gate_bypass: "coverage") is removed. All COVERAGE.md tickers produce `MODEL_READY=YES` through correctly calibrated thresholds in `/stock-signal`. The gate enforces uniformly for all tickers.
1104	69. **MODEL_READY=NO refusal unchanged →** invoking `/stock-model` on any ticker with `model_ready=NO` emits the standard refusal regardless of whether the ticker is in COVERAGE.md.
1105	70. **`gate_bypass` field retired →** `stages.model.gate_bypass` is no longer emitted. Existing report files may contain the field; it is ignored on read.

340	
341	**Report JSON write:**
342	Run `mkdir -p reports` before writing.
343	Write to `reports/TICKER_YYYYMMDD.json` where YYYYMMDD is today's date.
344	
345	Merge behaviour:
346	- If the file already exists (e.g. written by `/stock-screen` earlier), READ it first, then merge — add/update `stages.signal` and `meta` fields. Do NOT overwrite `stages.screen` or other stages.
347	- If the file does not exist, create it with the full outer structure.
348	
349	**Required JSON structure for a new file:**
350	
351	```json
352	{
353	  "ticker": "[TICKER]",
354	  "company": "[company name or ticker if unavailable]",
355	  "date": "[YYYY-MM-DD]",
356	  "stages": {
357	    "signal": {
358	      "verdict": "[BUY | WATCH | CAUTION]",
359	      "profit_stage": "[ESTABLISHED | EMERGING]",
360	      "track": "[GROWTH | YIELD]",
361	      "ai_layer": "[INFRASTRUCTURE | APPLICATION | MODEL | INCUMBENT | N/A | null]",
362	      "clean_eps_ttm": "[number or null]",
363	      "sbc_stripped": "[true | false | null]",
364	      "sbc_adjustment_per_share": "[number or null]",
365	      "peg_ratio": "[number or null]",
366	      "ps_ratio": "[number]",
367	      "rule_of_40": null,
368	      "qualitative": "[PASS | FLAG | FAIL]",
369	      "qualitative_note": "[one-line reason]",
370	      "signal": "[BUY | WATCH | CAUTION]",
371	      "model_ready": "[YES | CONDITIONAL | NO]",
372	      "condition": "[string or null]"
373	    }
374	  },
375	  "meta": {
376	    "profit_stage": "[ESTABLISHED | EMERGING]",
377	    "track": "[GROWTH | YIELD]",
378	    "ai_layer": "[classification or null]",
379	    "confidence": "[HIGH | MEDIUM | LOW]"
380	  }
381	}
382	```
383	
384	**Confidence rules:**
385	- HIGH: SBC stripped successfully + PEG computed (not N/A) + qualitative = PASS
386	- MEDIUM: any of SBC/PEG are N/A but P/S is available
387	- LOW: P/S is the only reliable data point
388	
389	**Field mapping from SIGNAL OUTPUT block to JSON:**
390	- Ticker → `ticker` (uppercase)
391	- Company → `company`
392	- Profit stage → `profit_stage` (and `meta.profit_stage`)
393	- Track → `track` (and `meta.track`)
394	- AI layer → `ai_layer` (and `meta.ai_layer`) — strip the rationale note; JSON gets the enum value only (e.g. `"INFRASTRUCTURE"`, not `"INFRASTRUCTURE — primary revenue from data centre GPUs..."`)
395	- Clean EPS (TTM) → `clean_eps_ttm` (number; `null` if N/A)
396	- SBC stripped → `sbc_stripped` (`true` if YES; `null` if N/A)
397	- SBC adjustment dollar value → `sbc_adjustment_per_share` (number; `null` if N/A)
398	- PEG ratio → `peg_ratio` (number; `null` if N/A)
399	- P/S ratio → `ps_ratio` (number)
400	- Rule of 40 → `rule_of_40` (`null` — deferred)
401	- Qualitative → `qualitative` (PASS/FLAG/FAIL only; rationale goes in `qualitative_note`)
402	- Signal → `signal` (and `verdict` — both must be the same value)
403	- MODEL_READY → `model_ready`
404	- Condition → `condition` (string; `null` if not CONDITIONAL)
405	
406	**JSON validity rules:**
407	- Use JSON `null` (not `""` or `0`) for any unavailable field
408	- No trailing commas, no comments, no NaN values
409	- `date` is ISO format `YYYY-MM-DD`
410	- `sbc_stripped` is a boolean (`true`/`false`/`null`) — never the string `"YES"` or `"N/A"`
411	
412	After writing, print:
413	
414	```

65. **Confidence anchor →** when playbook sets `confidence_anchor: MEDIUM`, the run's `meta.confidence` is capped at MEDIUM even when all other inputs are MCP-sourced and would yield HIGH. Cap is one-way (never raises); binding over runtime-computed confidence.
66. **Playbook validation →** a playbook with malformed YAML frontmatter halts the run with a clear error message before any MCP call. Unknown frontmatter fields emit a per-field warning and do not fail the load.

Good data. Computing the Signal manually following the SKILL:

- **Profit stage:** PE 22.23 > 0 → **ESTABLISHED**
- **AI layer:** META → **INCUMBENT** (ads core; AI = optionality)
- **AI optionality (INCUMBENT):** TTM capex $69.7B × ~65% AI = $45.3B → ×5 proxy = $226.5B / $1551.5B market cap = **14.6%** → proportionate (≤ 20%)
- **SBC strip:** SBC $20.43B / 2.196B shares = $9.30/sh per-share SBC. Reported EPS $27.50 − $9.30 = **Clean EPS $18.20**
- **PEG:** Clean fwd P/E = $611.21 / ($34.73 − $9.30) = $611.21/$25.43 = **24.04**. eps_growth_5y null → fallback g = (34.73/18.20)^(1/5) − 1 = **13.8%** (floor doesn't trigger). PEG = 24.04/13.8 = **1.74**
- **Threshold (INCUMBENT):** PEG 1.74 ≤ 4.0 → **WATCH** (just over BUY 1.5)
- **Qualitative:** PASS (optionality proportionate)
- **MODEL_READY:** **YES** (Signal WATCH, ESTABLISHED, no overrides)
